S . O . 2453 ..- - Whereas by notification of the Covernment 
of India in the Ministry of Petroloum and Natural Gas 
No. 779 dalel 27- 2 -90 under sub-section (1 ) of Section 3 of 
The Peroleum and Mincrals l ipelincy (Acquisition of Right 
of User in Lund ) Act, 1962 ( 50 1 1962 ) , the Central 
Government declared its intention to acquiro the right of 
user in lands specified in the schedule appended to that 
Nutification for the purpose of laying pipeline . 


और मागे, यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर 
विचार करने के पश्चात् इस अधिसुचना से मलग्न अनुसूची 
में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने 
का विनिश्चय किया है । 


And whereas the Competent Authority has under gub 
vection ( 1 of the Section 6 of the said Act, submitted report 
to the Government ; 


And further where y the Central Government has, after 
considering the said report decided to acquire the right of 
user in the lands in the schedulo appended to this noti 
fication ; 


अब , अतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा 
( 1 ) वारा प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एतदद्वारा घोषित करती है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न 
अनुसूची में विनिदिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार 
पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए एतद दाग अजित 
किया जाता है । 


Now therefore , in exercise of the power conferred by sub 
section ( 1) of Section 6 of the said Act , the Central 
Government hereby declares that the right of user in the 
said lands specified in the schedule appended to this noti 
fication hereby acquired for laying the pipeline ; 


और आगे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करत केहए न्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है 
कि उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में 
निहित होने की बजाए नल और प्राकृतिक गम प्रायोग में , 
सभी बाधाओं से मुक्त रूप में , घोषणा के प्रकाशन की इस 
सारीख को निहित होगा । 


And further in exerciso of power conferred by sub- stction 
( 4 ) of the section, the Central Government directs that the 
rights of user in the said lands shali instcad of vesting in 
Contral Government vests on this dato of the publication of 
this declaration in the Oil and Natural Gas Commission 
free from encumbrances.

प्राधिकारी होगा और स्तम्भ 3 में विनिदिष्ट प्राधिकारी स्तम्भ :1 में विनिर्दिष्ट शास्तियों की बाबम अनानाममिक प्राधिकारी होगा :

से पनल के सदस्य नहीं रहेंगे ।

12 . The fact that Gaikwad had appeared for the Workman 
on the last 3 dates, does not , in the peculiar circumstances 
of the presem case , make any difference . Gaikwad could 
do nothing for the Workman . On the last date , the Enquiry 
Ollicer shorted the unusual course of exhibiting documents, 
one after live oiler , without examining any witness connected 
With !!Cl. The ser ol sness of the questions involved was 
not sifficiently precent to his mind . He should have granted 
the Workman s sequest for Lawyer, although it had techni 
cally coine late . On the peculiar facts of the case , I am 
impressed by the Management s submission that Caikwad s 
acritaince should be treated. as adequate . 

13. The result of the above discussion is that in the present 
case scrious prejudice has resulted to the Workman by the 
Enqury Officer s declining the request of the Workman to 
engage a lawyer . In the peculiar and special circumstances of 
the case , the Workman was very much entitled to a Lawyer s 
assistance. Indeed the prejudice caused is so gross that in 
the words used in Furtado s case , there has been a failure 
of the inquiry itself. Findings on serious charges have been 
reached in absence of legal evidence . Indeed I would have 
heen inclined to treat the inquiry as had , even de hors the 
factor of lawyer s assistance . 

14 . The domestic inquiry is held to be had in law . As 
the Manarement have reserved their option in their written 
statement to lead evidence on the merits of the charges, hoth 
-ides will be entitled to lead evidence before the Trib ;inal. 
Costs on far to abide the final result.

का . आ . 2463: - -- औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम 
1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 17 के अनुसरण में 
केन्द्रीय सरकार बिडला सीमेंट वर्कम , चनापत्थर खान , 
चन्देरिया चित्तौड़गढ़ ( राजस्थान ) के प्रबन्धतंत्र के संबद्ध नियोजकों 

और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच, अनबंध में निर्दिष्ट औद्योगिक 
विवाद में औद्योगिक अधिकरण , उदयपर के पंचाट को 
प्रकाशित करती है, जो केन्द्रीय सरकार को 24- 8- 90 को 
प्राप्त हुअा था । 

S .O . 2463.-- In pursuance of Section 17 of the Industrial 
Dissutes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Central Government 
hereby publishes the award of the Indusrtial Tribunal, Udai 
pur as shown in the Annexure, in the indusrtial dispute bet 
ween the employers in relation to the management of Birla 
Cement Works, Limestone Mine, Chinderia , Chit: orgarh , 
( Raasthan ) and their workmen , which was received by the 
Central Government on 24 - 8 - 90 . 


प्रार्थी को विपक्षी द्वारा संवा मुक्ति घरेल जांच पर 
अाधारित होने से न्यायालय को सर्वप्रथम घरेल जांच 
की वैधानिकता के बाबत निश्चय करना था , लेकिन 11- 6- 90 
को प्रार्थी के प्रतिनिधि ने धरेल जांच की वैधानिकता को 
कोई चुनौती नहीं दी . लेकिन पारोपित आरोप व नथ्यों 
के संदर्भ में दंड को अत्याधिक कठोर व गैर अनपातिक व 
ज्मादतीपूर्ण बनाते हये इसमें न्यायोचित रियायत का अनरोध 
किया , जबकि विपक्षी प्रतिनिधि ने इसका घोर विरोध किया । 


घरेलू जांच जिस बावन की गई उसके बाबत प्रार्थी पर 
प्रागेप यह था कि प्रार्थी दिनांक 7- 10- 87 को ए शिफ्ट

चालक भी है और पूर्व में भी कई चालकों द्वारा ऐसी ही

कि मौजूदा घटना के समय सामने से अन्य वाहन के पाने 
के कारण यह घटना घटित हुई है और वास्तव में

मैने इस संदर्भ में समझौता अधिकारी की रिपोर्ट का 
अवलोकन किया कि जिममें समझौता अधिकारी द्वारा विपक्षी 
को यह सुझाव दिया गया था कि प्रार्थी को नौकरी से निकालने

नीची श्रेणी पर नियुक्ति दे दी जाय लेकिन विपक्षी प्रबन्धक

मेरी राय में विपक्षी को समझौता अधिकारी का यह 
सुझाव मान लेना चाहिये था क्योंकि मेरी राय में उक्त

जाना न्यायोचित प्रतीत होता है ।

i ording to the Unioll , the chargesheet issued and 
the onury Cedings held against the said worker are 
tud in law , and the indings of the Enquiry Officer are 
perare. I find ikat it is not so . According to the workman , 
tlie chargesheets were issued against him on three different 
ocasiois. However , a copy of only one chargesheet dated 
2 - 7 - 1984 (Ex . 8 ) has been produced before this Tribunal. 
Chuge . Nos. 1 and 2 mentioned therein are as stated at 
the beginning above . in that chargesheet it was stated that 
the man .. gerrent proposed to rely upon the workman s 
attendance card , Dity Roaster for the period from 1 - 4 - 1984 
to 30 - 6 - 1984 and the Report dated 18 - 6 - 1984 of Shri O . P . 
Bimbrabw , Chief Plant Engineer in support of the charges 
levelled against him . It is further seen that a copy of the 
Repoil dated 18 -6 - 1984 was sent to the workman along 
wit ite chargesheet. He was further told that in case he 
wanted to put in written statement in reply to the charges 
he should submit it within seven days, failing which it would 
be pre -ui ed that he did not want to put in any written 
statement. Ex . 9 are the copies of the enquiry proceedings . 
It is seen therefrom that the workman Shri U . D . Yadav 
had petticinaied in the proceedings . It is seen from those 
enquiry proceedings that he admitted before the Enquiry 
Oficer that he understood the charges framed against him 
and they were very clear to him . It is seen that two wit 
nesses were examined on behalf of the management, namely , 
Shri U Rama Rao , Office Superintendent , and Shri O . P . 
Biznahi , Chief Plant Officer . Both these witnesses were 
cross- examined by the workman himself . The workman also 
examined one witness on his behalf, namely Shri P . K . 
Wars. He was also cross -examined on behalf of the manage 
ment. After the recording of evidence was over, the work 
man filed his submissions in writing before the Enquiry Offi 
cer . Thereafter , the Enquiry Officer submitted his report in 
the matter . I. therfore , find that the chargesheet issued 
against the workman and the enquiry proceedings held 
against him were quite as per law and were not bad in law . 
Teslie No. 1 is therefore found in the negative . Ac 
cording to the Union , the findings of the Enquiry 
Officer are serverse . The said witnesses for the 
management deposed in support of the case of the manage 
ment. The witness Shri Wagh examined on behalf of the 
workman deposed in support of the case of the workman 
namely , that the workman had attended his duty on 18th . 
19th and 20th June, 1984 and that he had not participated 
in the illegal strike , nor had he done any illegal acts on 
any of these threc days. As per the evidence of the manage 
ment witness Shri Rama Rao before the Enquiry Officer , 
thic workiman had attended the duty on the 18th instant, 
but on 19th instant he was marked absent and had not 
worked on that dav, and that he had also not reported for 
dutor on the 20th instant. As per the evidence of the other 
mangement witness Shri Bimrahw , on 18 -6 - 1984 the work 
mom was among non -working helners . As per the evidence 
of the workman s witness Shri Wagh , he had allotted the 
duty to the workman from 18 - 6 - 84 to 20 - 6 - 1984 , and that 
the workman had attended his duty accordingly .

18th June, 1984 he also participated in the mob , 
who tried to prevent the Technicians from re 
pairing the G .S . Equipment and also shouted and 
gesticulated in such a manner that violence was 
imminent . 

In view of the above , I hold Shri Yaday guilty 
of the charges under Clanse 6 ( c ) and sub - clauses 
9 , 18 , 19 and 2n of Clause 28 of the Standing 
Orders applicable to him and also interference 

with the work of other employees . 
According to the Union . the said findings of the Enquiry 
Officer are preverse . It may be noted that while 
considering the evidence of the said witnesses the Enquiry 
Officer has also taken into consideration the evidence of 
workmin s witness Shr Wagh in his cross -examination and 


Thus, the name of Shri U . D . Yadav is appearing in this 
complaint among other persons who had taken part in the 
illegal acts committed on that day . There was no reason 
Why Shri Bimrahw should have lodged a false complaint, as 
above , against the said workmen on the very day of that 
incident. Therefore , taking into consideration this coniplaint 
and the evidence of the two management witnesses and the 
statements made hy the workman s witness Shri Wagh in his 
cross - examination , was the conclusions arrived at by the 
Enquiry Officer could not be termed as perverse . I find that 
the findings of the Enquiry Officer are just and proper . Issue 
No. 2 is therefore found in the negative . 
ISSUFS NOS . 4 and 5 

9 . As such the action of the management of Indian Airlines 
in relation to its establishment at Bombay in imposing the 
punishment of stoppage of one increment with effect from 
1- 8 - 1985 on Shri U . D . Yadav, Helper is just and proper . 
Issue No. 4 is found in the affirmative . As such , thc worman 
concerned is not entitled to any relief. Issue No . 5 is found 
accordingly . 


ISSUES NOS. 6 and 7 


10 . It is not disputed that the management had paid 50 % 
of the wages to the worman of the period of his suspension . 
According to the Union , as the suspension was withdrawn , 
the worman was entitled to full wages as per the provisions 
of the model Standing Orders framed under the Industrial 
Employment ( Standing Orders ) Act , 1946 . Howevei , as no 
ied arove , the employees of the said inanagement are gover 
ned by the Standing Orders framed under the Air Corporation 
Act, 1953, and not by the Standing Orders framed under 
the Industrial Employment ( Standing Orders ) Act 1946 . 
According to the management as per the provisions of the 
standing orders as they apply to the said workman , he is not 
entitled to full wages of the suspension period , but is entitled 
to only 1 2 of the wages. I find that the said contention 
of the management is quite tenable in law . Therefore, the 
allegation of Air Corporation Employees Union , Bombay , 
that the management of Indian Airlines, Bombay , have not 
made due payment of subsistence allowance and other dues 
frcal 20 -6 -1984 to 6 - 7 - 1984 to Shri U . D . Yadav, Helder is 
not correct. Issue No. 6 is therefore found in the negative . 
As such the workman is not entitled to any relef. Issue No 
1 is found accordingly . 

u . It is true that before the punishment of stoppage of 
one increment was imposed upon the workman , on notice 
to show cause why such a punishment should not be imposed 
upon him , was issued to him . However, the punishemat 
proposed to be imposed was only a minor one, and as such 
no injustice has been caused to the workman by not issuing 
show cause notice to him ,

While computing the work actually done I have also 
kept in mind the principles laid down referred to in 
paras 25 and 26 of the award , 

30 . It has further to be pointed out that the work 
man had served the Eunk ſor 5 -4 days in the year 
1965 also and this period has been ignored on account 
of the technicalities. 

31. That apart , the manner in which the workman 
concerned was cmployeć shows that the wurkman was 
available as and when required . In the case of H . D . 
Singh ( supra ) thc Tikka Mazdoor has workeil for 4 
days in 1974 , 154 days from January to December 
1975 and 105 days from January to July 1976 . Thus 
lle worked for 202 days from July 1975 to July 1976 
and adding 52 Sundays and 17 Holidays the total 
number of days on which worked werc 271 days . It 
is true that there is little distinction in this case in 
asnuch as the workman used to report to the Bank 
regularly at 9 . 30 a . m , as per terms of the contract to 
ascertain whether he would get work on every day . 
On days when no work was given he had to wait till 
moon to be told by the authorities concerned that no 
work was illitatile on Slich days . In this case ul] 
Sundays and Holidays from July 1975 to July 1976 
were included for computing 240 days of continuous 
Service . 

32 . Despite certain dissimilarity in the instant case 
with the case of H . D . Singh ( supra ) the substance 
is that in the instant case the workman was available 
as and when cosired which fact has not only been 
pleaded by him hut has not been controverted lay the 
mana rement. Otherwise also the manner in wrich he 
was given work can certainly lead to a reasonable 
conclusion that unfair labour practice was adopted to 


15 , 1990 /FETT 24, 1912 

4149 
- - - - -- - -- - - - - -- --- - 
see that he dous 119t complete 240 days as has been 
discussed in the case ol H . D . Singh ( supra ) in para 
13 of the judgment and deliberate breaks are apparent. 

33. Ihus without ſuriher ado we rcach to the cen 
clusion that the workman has swined into the harbour 
of Section 25F and he cannot be retrenched withiut 
paynient at the uime of retrenchment compensation 
compuici as prescribed therein road with Section 
25B12 ), Thus the retrenchment is void ab initio and 
therefore he is entitled 10 bo reinstated ( See Mohan 
Lal Vs, Bharat Electronics Ltd , ( 1981) 3 SCC 225 
& N . Sunderamoney supra ) . 

34 . Now the question is as to from what date he 
should be deemed to be reinstated and what further 
relicf 10 should be granted . The workman , as adinitled 
by niru , was appearing in various tests and interviews 
and when he could not succecd ultimately sougit the 
resort of this Court, He was retrenhced as bask as on 
1 - 1 - 1967. The reference was made on 26th August , 
1981. While his case was pending before this Tribunal, 
to management considered his case as per Ex. M . 30 
and the Agreement as per Ex. M |31, but according 
to the inanagement he had not completed 240 days 
of continuous service and therefore he was not given 
the employment. This was obviously done after tho 
reference was made. Thus there is substantial force in 
the version of the management that the workman 
concerned was sleeping over his right for more than 
a decade. There is , however, no period of limitation 
prescribed . The relerence has been made. The terms 
of settlement, as put Ex . M 31 have not been en 
forced in his case . Thus these terms of settlement have 
to be ignored for the purpose of this individual case 
in the above circumstances. 

25 . Order of termination being void ab initio the 
workijän would be deemed to be in continuous service 
from 1 - 1 -1967 onwards but in the facts and circum 
stances of this case he will not be entitled to any 
nack wage ; and other monetary benefits. He will, how 
ever, be entitled to his seniority from 1 - 1 - 1967 on 
wards and he be placed accordingly as per ruleg of 
the managenient and other benefits arising therefrom . 
He will , however , be entitled to wages from the date 
of award if he is not serving anywhere during this 
period or from the date of joining as the case may 
be. I record my findings accordingly and reference is 
answered as follows : 

The action of the management of the State 
Bank of India Seoni Br. in terminating the 
services of Shri M . L . Raichandani, Cashier 
cum - Clerk w .c .f. 1 - 1 - 1967 is not justified . 
He is entitled to be reinstated from 1 - 1 - 1967 
and siall be deemed to be in continuous 
service from 1 -1 - 1967 onwards; the manage 
ment shall give him seniority accordingly and 
other benefits arising therefrom , 
He will, however, be entitled to wages from 
the date of the award if he is not serving 
anywhere during this period or from the 
date of joining as the case may be . 
No order as to costs,

14 . With regard to Kamlesh Montessory School, 
the E .O , has observed as follows at page 3 of the 
inquiry report 
In his cross examination Shri Sareen has admit 

ted that Kamlesh Montessory School is 
functioning in the house acquired by Shri 
Sareen with the aid of Housing Loan from 
the bank since 1985 and he did not seek 
any permission from the bank in this regard 
or gave any notice to the bank . . . . . . . . 
I exoperate Shri Sareen of the charge that 
he is being paid some monthly rent by the 
said Nursery School, however , Sh . Sareen 
in his cross examination has stated that his 
is a joint family comprising of his mother, 
brother , unmarried sister , wife and children 
and they are living in the same house . His 
living in a joint family alongwith his mother 
and brother who are running the nursery 
school lie must be the beneficiary from the 
income of the school indirectly , if not direc 
ly . . . . Shri Shanti Ranjan M .W . 1 in his 
deposition has stated that the school has 
more than 100 children and 3 /4 tcachers 
are working there . Students are charged 
fee and other dues . The defence did not 
cross examine the M . W . 1 , on this issue 
and as such it is established that Shri Sareen 
is using the house acquired by him with 
the help of housing loan for commercial 
purposes in vivlation of Housing Loan Ru 
les of the bank . 


has not named his brother in the said application .

Therefore, this charge fully stands proved . 

15 . Thus the findings given by th » [ . O . with regard

POINT NO . 2 

16 . Looking to the nature of the charges , the 
punishment awarded cannot be called as dispropor 
tionate or excessive . From the rules governing the 
grant of housing loans it appcars that the object of 
the scheme is 10 assist an employee wlio does not 
own any resideniial house . It further appears that 
the loan for house granted at a comparatively low 
interest. The rates of interest are given at page 3 of 
the Rules . Interest at commercial rate is certainly 
much higher . The purpose will stand defeated if in 
tho garb of acquiring the residential house with the 
help of housing loan an employec tries to run a trade 
or business in ir either himself or through one or 
some of the members of his family . On point No. 2 
also the Union workman files . 

17. Hence, it is held that the punishment of 
stoppage of iwo annual increments with cumulative 
effect having the eflect of postponing his future in 
crements awarded by the management of Union Bouk 
of Indian cannot be held in violation of the principles 
of natural justice , Conscquently , the workinan is held 
entitled to do nelief,
